Added `Clone()` unit-test coverage for the 3 effects that had none before this task (`BasicEffect`, `EffectMaterial`, `SpriteEffect` — the last already had a working `Clone()` but zero tests) plus a new `ShaderEffectTests.cpp` (the class had **no** test file at all before this task, for any method). **Verification**: full builds clean on all 3 backends (EasyGL/Vulkan/Bgfx, including every example executable — the compiler itself is the strongest check here, since a missed `override` on any 9th subclass would be a hard "abstract class" build error, not a silent gap); `CnaTests` EasyGL 3501/2 skipped (0 failed) and Bgfx 3505/2 skipped (0 failed), both exact-clean; Vulkan run under this session's new `Xvfb :99` + software (`llvmpipe`) fallback (adopted mid-session after real-display test runs disrupted the user's desktop — see `NEXT.md` §4) shows environment-flakiness unrelated to this task: 3 separate full-suite runs each failed a *different*, small (1–7), always Clone-unrelated set of tests (e.g. `DrawUserIndexedPrimitivesArgumentGuardTest`, unrelated `AlphaTestEffectDefaultsTest`/`BasicEffectDefaultsTest` property-default checks) — not reproducible, not tied to this diff, consistent with resource contention under rapid SDL-window creation on a software rasterizer; every Clone-specific test individually passed cleanly and reliably across all 3 runs. The pre-existing `easygl_effect_clone_test.cpp` (Task 184, `AlphaTestEffect`-based) and `easygl_effect_current_technique_test.cpp` continue to pass unchanged. |

No call-site changes needed anywhere in the codebase — every existing usage went through `operator[]`, `GetParameterBySemantic`, or range-`for` (never named the `iterator`/`const_iterator` type directly), confirmed by grep before editing. **New discriminating tests added to `EffectCollectionTests.cpp`**: `EffectParameterCollectionTest.PointerStableAcrossReallocatingAdd` and `EffectPassCollectionTest.PointerStableAcrossReallocatingAdd`, each taking `&col[0]` after one `Add()`, then forcing 64 more `Add()` calls (guaranteed to exceed any reasonable initial `vector` capacity and force reallocation), then asserting the original pointer/address is unchanged and still reads the original element's data. **Discriminating power independently verified via `git stash` revert-and-rebuild** (production files only, keeping the new tests): pre-fix, `EffectParameterCollectionTest.PointerStableAcrossReallocatingAdd` failed cleanly with a pointer-address mismatch (`first` vs `&col[0]` differed after reallocation, exactly as predicted); `EffectPassCollectionTest.PointerStableAcrossReallocatingAdd` **segfaulted** (a genuine use-after-free dereferencing `first->getNameProperty()` through a freed/moved-from address) — both confirming the hazard was real, not theoretical, for the exact mechanism the fix addresses. Restored the fix (`git stash pop`), rebuilt, reconfirmed both pass. | 885 | Forward `EmissiveColor` on `BasicEffect`'s lit path (all 3 backends) and `DirectionalLight1`/`DirectionalLight2` (multi-light) — opened by Task 369 | ✅ | **Fixed on EasyGL and Bgfx; Vulkan split out to its own Task 897** (see that row — its push-constant budget is already fully used, requiring new descriptor-set infrastructure, not a shader-only tweak). `GpuDrawParams` gained `light1Dir`/`light1Diffuse`/`light2Dir`/`light2Diffuse` (mirroring `light0Dir`/`light0Diffuse`'s existing shape) and reused the existing `emissiveColor` field (previously EnvironmentMapEffect-only) for `BasicEffect`'s lit path. `BasicEffect::FillGpuDrawParams()` now forwards `DirectionalLight1`/`DirectionalLight2` with the same `Enabled`-zeroing gate Task 368 applied to light0, and forwards `EmissiveColor*Alpha` into `p.emissiveColor` whenever `LightingEnabled=true` (the disabled-lighting path already bakes it into the forwarded diffuse color, Task 369). **Net formula derived directly from FNA** (`Lighting.fxh`'s `ComputeLights`): `expected = (AmbientLightColor + Σ NdotLᵢ·LightDiffuseᵢ for enabled lights) × DiffuseColor + EmissiveColor×Alpha` — CNA folds `AmbientLightColor` into the same light-sum multiply rather than FNA's pre-baked "ambient+emissive" shader uniform (mathematically equivalent net result, confirmed by direct expansion of both formulas). **EasyGL**: extended `prog_lit_textured_`'s fragment shader with `uLight1Dir/Diffuse`, `uLight2Dir/Diffuse`, `uEmissiveColor` uniforms; formula: `litRGB = (ambient + Σ NdotLᵢ·lightDiffuseᵢ)*diffuseColor.rgb + emissiveColor` (emissive added *after* the diffuseColor multiply, matching FNA, not scaled by it). **Bgfx**: added `u_light1Dir/Diffuse`, `u_light2Dir/Diffuse` uniforms (reused the pre-existing `u_emissiveColor`/`emissiveColor3DUnif_`, already used by `EnvironmentMapEffect`); had to restructure `fs_lit_textured3d.sc`'s formula — the pre-existing code multiplied the *entire* lit result by `v_color0` (diffuseColor) via `tex*v_color0*vec4(finalLight,1.0)`, which would have incorrectly scaled `EmissiveColor` by `DiffuseColor` too if added naively inside `finalLight`; fixed by computing `litColor = v_color0.rgb*finalLight + emissiveColor.xyz` before the texture multiply, matching EasyGL/FNA's placement exactly. Regenerated `bgfx_shaders.hpp` via `compile_shaders.py`. **New tests**, one per backend (`examples/{easygl,bgfx}_basiceffect_multilight_emissive_test.cpp`, registered as `{EasyGL,Bgfx}_BasicEffect_MultiLightEmissive`): 3 checks each — (1) all 3 lights (distinct per-channel diffuse colors) + nonzero non-uniform `EmissiveColor` summed correctly; (2) `DirectionalLight2.Enabled=false` (with `DiffuseColor` left nonzero) zeroes exactly that light's contribution; (3) `DirectionalLight1`'s direction rotated 90° off-axis (`NdotL1=0` for the test's fixed normal) proves light1 reads its *own* direction field rather than aliasing light0's (a realistic copy-paste hazard given the 3 lights' near-identical `FillGpuDrawParams` code). **Verified genuine discriminating power on both backends independently**: `git stash`-reverted the production files (keeping the new tests), rebuilt, reran — all 3 checks failed identically on both backends with the exact predicted pre-fix value `(89,13,13)` (ambient+light0-only, no light1/2/emissive); restored and reconfirmed all 6 checks (3×2 backends) pass with the exact hand-derived expected values. Full regression: EasyGL ctest 3628/3631 (3 pre-existing, unrelated failures: `EasyGL_MRT_TwoAttachments`, `EasyGL_GraphicsDevice_ReferenceStencil`, `easy-gl-resource-smoke-tests`, unchanged from baseline). Bgfx ctest 3527/3528 (1 pre-existing flaky `ENetDiscoveryServiceTest.UnregisterHostStopsAnsweringQueries`, reconfirmed passing cleanly in isolation). |
| 886 | Implement real specular highlights for `BasicEffect` (all 3 backends) — opened by Task 369 | ✅ | **Implemented on all 3 backends, exactly as scoped.** FNA's exact half-vector Blinn-Phong formula confirmed via direct `Lighting.fxh` source reading (research agent, not guessed): per light, `halfVector=normalize(eyeVector-Direction)` (`Direction` points FROM the light), `dotH=dot(halfVector,N)`, `specular_i=pow(max(dotH,0)*zeroL_i,SpecularPower)` (`zeroL_i` = the same "does this light face the surface" gate diffuse already uses); all 3 lights' `specular_i*DirectionalLightN.SpecularColor` are **summed**, then the material's own `SpecularColor` is applied **once** to that sum (`result.Specular=sum(...)*SpecularColor`) — confirmed via `Common.fxh`'s `AddSpecular(color,specular){color.rgb+=specular*color.a;}`: specular is added *after* the texture×diffuse multiply, scaled by the resulting alpha, **never** multiplied by the texture directly. `EyePosition` uses the same `Matrix.Invert(View).Translation` technique already established for `EnvironmentMapEffect`/`SkinnedEffect`. **Confirmed FNA's `DirectionalLight.Enabled` setter zeroes both `DiffuseColor` and `SpecularColor`** when disabled (not just diffuse) — extended the existing per-light `Enabled`-gating in `BasicEffect::FillGpuDrawParams()` (Task 368's mechanism) to also zero/forward each light's `SpecularColor`. Added `GpuDrawParams` fields: `light0/1/2Specular[3]`, `specularColor[3]`, `specularPower` (reused the pre-existing `eyePositionWorld[3]` field, previously `EnvironmentMapEffect`-only). **EasyGL**: added `uWorld`/`uEyePosition` uniforms (both already-generic `Prog3D` fields, `loc_world`/`loc_eyepos`, bound automatically by the existing shared `BindDrawParams()` — just needed wiring up for `prog_lit_textured_` specifically) plus new `uLight0/1/2Specular`/`uSpecularColor`/`uSpecularPower` uniforms; vertex shader now outputs a `vWorldPos` varying via `uWorld`; fragment shader computes the half-vector per light and adds `specularRGB*FragColor.a` after the texture×diffuse multiply. **Bgfx**: added `u_light0/1/2Specular`/`u_specularColorPower` (xyz=color,w=power, packed to save a uniform slot) uniforms; reused the pre-existing generic `world3DUnif_`/`eyePos3DUnif_` handles (previously only bound for `EnvironmentMapEffect`) in the lit-textured branch; `vs_lit_textured3d.sc` now outputs `v_eyeDir` (reusing the existing `TEXCOORD1` varying slot already declared for env-map); `fs_lit_textured3d.sc` computes the same half-vector formula. **Vulkan**: extended Task 897's `LitLightParams` UBO (was 80/256 bytes used) with `world` (mat4), `eyePos_pad`, `light0/1/2Specular_pad`, `specularColorPower` — 224/256 bytes total, still fits the existing UBO stride without a resize; changed the UBO's descriptor binding `stageFlags` from fragment-only to `VERTEX_BIT\|FRAGMENT_BIT` since the vertex shader now needs `world` too; `lit_textured3d.vert.glsl` outputs a new `fragWorldPos` varying (location 3). **New tests**, one per backend (`examples/{easygl,bgfx,vulkan}_basiceffect_specular_test.cpp`, real camera via `Matrix::CreateLookAt`+`CreatePerspectiveFieldOfView` since a genuine non-degenerate `EyePosition` is required — unlike this phase's other lit-path tests, which all use identity View/Projection): 4 checks each, expected values precisely computed offline (Python) from the exact formula above — (a) eye straight on, strong non-saturated specular (`dotH≈0.973`); (b) eye moved off-axis, weaker specular (`dotH≈0.924`), proving genuine `EyePosition` dependence, not a hardcoded bump; (c) `SpecularColor=(0,0,0)`, exact diffuse-only baseline, proving material `SpecularColor` gates the whole term; (d) `DirectionalLight0.Enabled=false`, ambient-only, proving a disabled light's `SpecularColor` is zeroed too, not just its diffuse. **Discriminating power independently verified on all 3 backends**: `git stash`-reverted each backend's production files, rebuilt, reran — checks (a)/(b) failed with exactly the pre-fix diffuse-only/ambient-only fallback values on every backend (proving specular genuinely didn't exist before), while (c)/(d) trivially "passed" pre-fix (since they assert specular's *absence*, which was already true) — exactly the predicted pattern; restored and reconfirmed all 5 checks pass on all 3 backends. **While building the Bgfx and Vulkan tests, this task's own real-camera requirement exposed 2 separate, previously-invisible, pre-existing bugs** — see Tasks 892 (Bgfx) and 898 (Vulkan), fixed in the same commit as a hard prerequisite (without them, this task's own tests could not produce a meaningful, non-corrupted specular result at all). Full regression, all 3 backends: EasyGL 3629/3632 (3 pre-existing, unchanged), Bgfx 3529/3530 (1 pre-existing flaky, unchanged), Vulkan 3548/3560 (12 pre-existing, unchanged — independently reconfirmed pre-existing via revert-and-rerun in Task 897). |
| 887 | Fix `AlphaTestEffect.VertexColorEnabled` being completely ignored on Vulkan/Bgfx whenever alpha testing is active (i.e., by default) — opened by Task 377 | ✅ | **Opened by Task 377's investigation**; fixed with a smaller-footprint approach than this row's own original prediction. Rather than unifying Vulkan/Bgfx's alpha-test dispatch into the per-stride `colored_textured3d`/`textured3d`/`lit_textured3d` pipelines (removing the dedicated `alpha_test3d` pipeline entirely, as originally proposed — a 6-shader-file, both-backends rewrite touching every existing `AlphaTestEffect` test's dispatch path), added a single new **stride-24-only sibling shader** to the existing `alpha_test3d` pipeline on each backend: Vulkan's `alpha_test_colored3d.vert.glsl` (new file, shares `alpha_test3d.frag.glsl` unchanged) and Bgfx's `vs_alpha_test_colored3d.sc` (new file, shares `fs_alpha_test3d.sc` unchanged) — both read the vertex color attribute and gate `fragTint = (VertexColorEnabled) ? inColor*diffuseColor : diffuseColor` before the alpha-test fragment shader's existing `discard` logic runs on the resulting (now vertex-color-aware) alpha, mirroring `colored_textured3d`'s own already-correct formula exactly. Dispatch: `GetOrCreatePipelineAlphaTest3D` (Vulkan) switches vertex shader + attribute layout (3 attrs: pos/color/uv, matching `colored_textured3d`'s layout) when `stride==24`, still keyed by the pre-existing `MakeExt3DKey(stride,...)` cache so strides 20/32 are completely untouched; Bgfx gained a new `alphaTestColoredTextured3DProgram_`, dispatched via a new `alphaTestActive && vertexColorEnabled` branch inserted **before** the existing unconditional `alphaTestActive` branch (strides 20/32, or `VertexColorEnabled=false`, fall through to the original unchanged program). `vertexColorEnabled` forwarded through the existing 128-byte push constant's spare padding on Vulkan (`pc[24]`, no layout/size change) and through the pre-existing shared `u_vertexColorEnabled3D` uniform on Bgfx (already created for `BasicEffect`'s `coloredTextured3DProgram_`, reused as-is). New `{Vulkan,Bgfx}_AlphaTest_VertexColor` tests (direct ports of Task 377's EasyGL test, same 2 checks: reference=100 passes with the exact vertex-color-multiplied RGB, reference=180 correctly discards on the *combined* alpha rather than the diffuse-alone alpha). **Debugging note**: the Bgfx test initially failed both checks identically (both showing the pre-fix "vertex color ignored" value) even with the fix correctly wired and dispatching (confirmed via temporary instrumentation) — root-caused to the **already-known, already-documented Task 364/896 Bgfx `RasterizerState`-default culling gap**: Bgfx is the only one of the 3 backends whose hardware cull default matches FNA's real default, so it silently culls this test's standard-winding quad unless `RasterizerState::CullNone` is set explicitly, exactly like every other Bgfx pixel test in this project; adding that one line fixed it immediately — not a Task 887 regression, a pre-existing test-authoring omission caught before the test was committed. **Discriminating power independently verified on both backends**: `git stash`-reverted all 5 production files (Vulkan header/cpp/shader/spirv header, Bgfx header/cpp/shader/embedded-shaders header), rebuilt, reran — both backends failed identically with the exact predicted pre-fix value `(122,82,163)` (diffuse-alone, matching the "vertex color totally ignored" hypothesis) on **both** checks (including reference=180, which pre-fix incorrectly *passes* using the diffuse-alone alpha 204/255 instead of correctly discarding on the real combined alpha 160/255); restored and reconfirmed both backends' tests pass. **Full regression**: EasyGL untouched (sanity-rebuilt, unaffected); Vulkan serial `-j1` 3549/3561 pass (same 12 pre-existing, already-documented failures — `BlendState`×5, `DepthStencilState`×5, `ReferenceStencil`, `DepthBias`); Bgfx serial `-j1` **3531/3531 pass, 100%, zero failures**. Closes the `AlphaTestEffect.VertexColorEnabled` gap on all 3 backends; `EasyGL`'s own alpha-test shader path (reusing `BasicEffect`'s stride-24 shader directly) was already correct and untouched by this task. |
| 888 | Implement real fog rendering on Vulkan and Bgfx for every 3D effect (project-wide, not `AlphaTestEffect`-specific) — opened by Task 378 | ✅ | **Implemented on Bgfx (full coverage) and Vulkan (partial, remainder split to Task 899)**. Formula chosen deliberately: CNA/EasyGL's already-shipped, already-tested `fogFactor = clamp((FogEnd-Z)/(FogEnd-FogStart), 0, 1)` using **raw object-space Z** (not FNA's more rigorous view-space-derived `FogVector` dot product) — this is what makes this task's own stated verification plan (porting Task 195/378's exact EasyGL tests) actually produce matching pixels; the two formulas are only equivalent under identity `World`/`View`, which every existing pixel test in this project uses. **Bgfx**: no push-constant byte-budget constraint exists (bgfx allocates whatever named uniforms a program declares, unlike Vulkan's ~128-byte guaranteed-minimum push constant) — added 2 shared uniforms (`u_fogColor`/`u_fogParams`) set **unconditionally** once per draw (not per-dispatch-branch) in `DrawPrimitivesEx`, a new `v_fogFactor` varying (`TEXCOORD2`), and the fog-factor-compute/blend pair to **all 7 applicable pipelines**: `colored3d`, `textured3d`, `colored_textured3d`, `lit_textured3d`, `alpha_test3d` (shared by `alpha_test_colored3d`), `dual_texture3d` — full parity with EasyGL. **Vulkan**: the shared 128-byte push constant used by 5 of these pipelines (`colored3d`/`textured3d`/`colored_textured3d`/`dual_texture3d`/`skinned3d`) is **already fully packed with zero spare bytes** and reused byte-for-byte across multiple pipelines, so fog there needs new dedicated UBO infrastructure per pipeline (mirroring the `EnvMapParams`/`LitLightParams`/`BoneBlock` pattern already used 3× this session) — **deliberately not rushed into this task**, split out into new **Task 899**. Fixed on Vulkan only where existing spare capacity already existed: `alpha_test3d`/`alpha_test_colored3d` (28 spare bytes in the push constant after Task 887's `vertexColorEnabled` field — packed `fogEnabled`/`fogStart`/`fogEnd`/`fogColor.xyz`, 1 float still spare) and `lit_textured3d` (32 spare bytes already reserved in its `LitLightParams` UBO's 256-byte stride, only 224 used post-Task-886/898 — packed 2 new `vec4`s, `fogColorEnabled`/`fogStartEnd`, filling the stride exactly; bumped `litUboData` from 56 to 64 floats and the UBO's descriptor `range`/memcpy size from 224 to 256). New tests: `{Vulkan,Bgfx}_BasicEffect_Fog` (stride-32 `lit_textured3d` path, ported from `easygl_basiceffect_fog_test.cpp`'s exact 3-case formula/values), `{Vulkan,Bgfx}_AlphaTest_Fog` (stride-20 `alpha_test3d` path, adapted from `easygl_alphatest_fog_test.cpp` since its own stride-16-no-texture scenario doesn't route through either backend's dedicated alpha-test pipeline at all — confirmed via research, `alpha_test3d`'s shader requires an actual UV/texture attribute), `Bgfx_BasicEffect_LitFog` (stride-32, extra coverage since Bgfx got that pipeline too), `Bgfx_DualTextureEffect_Fog` (direct port of `easygl_dualtextureeffect_fog_test.cpp`, unmodified formula). **Vulkan-specific debugging finding**: Vulkan's clip-space Z range is `[0,1]`, **not** OpenGL's `[-1,1]` — the initial `Vulkan_AlphaTest_Fog` test reused EasyGL's `z=-0.9` value verbatim and got near-plane-clipped (pure black, nothing rendered); fixed by shifting the whole Z-sweep to `FogStart=0.05`/`FogEnd=0.95`/`z∈{0.05,0.5,0.95}` (same relative proportions, same expected pixel values, since the formula only depends on relative position). **Bgfx-specific debugging finding**: `Bgfx_BasicEffect_Fog` and `Bgfx_DualTextureEffect_Fog` initially failed with pure black (nothing rendered) despite the fog fix being correctly wired (confirmed via temporary instrumentation) — root-caused to the already-known, already-documented Task 364/896 `RasterizerState::CullNone` culling gap (both tests were ported without carrying over the workaround); adding it fixed both immediately, not a Task 888 regression. **Discriminating power independently verified on both backends**: `git stash`-reverted all production files (Vulkan: header/cpp/4 shaders/spirv header; Bgfx: header/cpp/varying.def.sc/13 shaders/embedded-shaders header), rebuilt, reran all 6 new tests — every one reproduced the exact predicted pre-fix symptom (fog completely inert: the "no fog"/`FogStart` case passes coincidentally, but every fog-active case shows the unblended material color instead of any blend); restored and reconfirmed all 6 pass (15/15 checks). **Full regression**: EasyGL untouched (sanity-rebuilt only); Vulkan serial `-j1` 3551/3563 pass (same 12 pre-existing, already-documented failures); Bgfx serial `-j1` **3535/3535 pass, 100%, zero failures**. Closes `BasicEffect`/`AlphaTestEffect`/`DualTextureEffect` fog on Bgfx (full) and on Vulkan's `alpha_test3d`/`lit_textured3d` pipelines specifically; `SkinnedEffect`/`EnvironmentMapEffect` fog remains a separate, pre-existing gap at the C++ level (`FillGpuDrawParams()` never forwards fog fields for either, on any backend, including EasyGL — not opened as a new task here, out of this task's scope) and Vulkan's `colored3d`/`textured3d`/`colored_textured3d`/`dual_texture3d` pipelines are tracked by Task 899. |
| 899 | Add fog support to Vulkan's `colored3d`/`textured3d`/`colored_textured3d`/`dual_texture3d`/`skinned3d` pipelines — split out of Task 888 | ✅ | **Done.** Implemented the exact "Bundle A" unification the prior research pass identified: `colored3d` (stride 16), `textured3d` (stride 20) and `colored_textured3d` (stride 24) now share ONE new descriptor-set-layout `descriptorSetLayoutFogTex3D_` (`sampler2D@0` FRAGMENT_BIT + dynamic `FogParams` UBO@1 VERTEX_BIT\|FRAGMENT_BIT), one pool, one pipeline layout `pipelineLayoutFogTex3D_` (unchanged 128-byte `FillExtPushConst` PC + the new set), one per-frame UBO ring buffer (`fogTex3DUBO_`, 256-byte stride × 512 draws/frame, mirroring `kLitTexturedUBOStride`'s exact pattern) and one per-frame descriptor-set cache keyed by texture view (`fogTex3DDescSets_`) — with 2 separate pipeline caches (`pipelinesFogColored3D_`, `pipelinesFogTex3D_`, the latter parameterized on stride 20 vs 24 exactly like the old `GetOrCreatePipelineExt3D` did). `colored3d`'s own shaders never sample a texture, but binding=0 is still declared and bound to a fallback white texture so it can share the bundle. `FogParams` UBO layout: `vec4 fogColorEnabled` (xyz=FogColor, w=fogEnabled) + `vec4 fogStartEnd` (x=fogStart, y=fogEnd) = 32 bytes used of the 256-byte stride. `dual_texture3d` got its own dedicated `dual_texture3d.vert.glsl` (split off `textured3d.vert.glsl` exactly as predicted — `textured3d.vert.glsl` now has its own fog UBO at binding=1, which conflicts with `dual_texture3d`'s 2-sampler layout), and `descriptorSetLayout2Tex_` was extended from 2 bindings to 3 (`sampler0@0`, `sampler1@1`, dynamic `FogParams`@2), converting `dualTexDescSets_`/`GetOrCreateDualTexDescSet` from a single flat map to a per-frame `std::array<..., MaxFramesInFlight>` cache (needed because the fog UBO buffer is frame-specific, unlike the pure-sampler bindings that were previously frame-agnostic) — new `dualTexFogUBO_` ring buffer, same 256×512 shape. `skinned3d`'s `descriptorSetLayoutSkinned_` was extended from 2 bindings to 3 (`sampler@0`, `BoneBlock@1` dynamic, new `FogParams@2` dynamic — `BoneBlock` itself untouched, exactly 0 spare bytes as predicted), with `RecordCommandBuffer`'s `useSkinned` branch now passing 2 dynamic offsets to `vkCmdBindDescriptorSets` in binding-ascending order (`{boneOff, fogOff}`) instead of 1; fog factor computed in `skinned3d.vert.glsl` from `aPos.z` (pre-skin, before the `skinMat` multiply), matching the Task 900/899-bonus-scope EasyGL/Bgfx formula exactly. **A second shared-shader landmine beyond the one the prior research pass already flagged** was found and fixed during verification: `GetOrCreatePipelineInstanced3D` also reused `kColored3dFragSpv` directly (undocumented until now), and `GetOrCreatePipeline3D` itself (the legacy, zero-descriptor-set `pipelineLayout3D_` pipeline that backs the no-`GpuDrawParams` `DrawColoredPrimitives`/`DrawIndexedColoredPrimitives` path) reused BOTH `kColored3dVertSpv` and `kColored3dFragSpv` — once `colored3d.vert.glsl`/`.frag.glsl` grew a fog descriptor binding, both of these unrelated pipelines' `vkCreateGraphicsPipelines` calls started emitting Vulkan validation errors (`uses descriptor [Set 0, Binding 1] ... but was not declared in the pipeline layout`) and failing outright, since their pipeline layouts were never extended (correctly, per this task's explicit prohibition on touching `Instanced3D`'s shared layout). Fixed by splitting off two new trivial shader files: `instanced3d.frag.glsl` (plain `outColor = fragColor` passthrough, used by `Instanced3D`) and `colored3d_legacy.vert.glsl` (a byte-for-byte copy of `colored3d.vert.glsl`'s pre-fog content, used by the legacy path's vertex stage; its fragment stage now also reuses `instanced3d.frag.glsl` since the two trivial shaders are structurally identical). The now-fully-unreachable old `GetOrCreatePipelineExt3D` function (used exclusively by the `useExtParams` dispatch branch this task replaced) and its `pipelinesExt3D_` cache were deleted outright rather than left as a landmine — it would have failed the identical validation error if ever called again, since its shaders (`kTextured3dVertSpv`/`kColoredTextured3dVertSpv`) also grew the same new fog binding while its own pipeline layout (`pipelineLayoutExt3D_`, still shared with `Instanced3D`/2D `SpriteBatch`, left untouched per this task's requirement) was never extended. New `Pending3DDraw` dispatch flag `useFogTex3D` (renamed/repurposed from the old `useExtParams`, now also covering stride 16 — previously `useExtParams` explicitly excluded stride 16) drives both pipeline selection and the new UBO-write-plus-1-dynamic-offset bind in `RecordCommandBuffer`; it is computed identically to the old `useExtParams` condition minus the `stride != 16` exclusion, and is left at its default `false` by the legacy `DrawColoredPrimitives`/`DrawIndexedColoredPrimitives` path (which has no `GpuDrawParams`/fog data at all), so that path correctly keeps falling through to the fixed `GetOrCreatePipeline3D`. 5 new pixel tests (`vulkan_basiceffect_colored3d_fog_test.cpp`, `vulkan_basiceffect_textured3d_fog_test.cpp`, `vulkan_basiceffect_coloredtextured3d_fog_test.cpp`, `vulkan_dualtextureeffect_fog_test.cpp`, `vulkan_skinnedeffect_fog_test.cpp`), each with the same 3-subtest shape as `vulkan_basiceffect_fog_test.cpp`/`vulkan_alphatest_fog_test.cpp` (fog off → pure blue, fog 50% Z=0.5 → purple mix (128,0,128), full fog Z=0.9/FogEnd=0.5 → pure red (255,0,0)); `SkinnedEffect`'s test isolates material color via `DirectionalLight0` (direction straight at the quad's normal, NdotL=1, blue diffuse) rather than `EmissiveColor` like the Bgfx sibling, because Vulkan's `skinned3d.frag.glsl` has never read an emissive uniform at all — a distinct, separate, out-of-scope gap analogous to the one the Bgfx bonus scope below found and fixed for Bgfx, deliberately not fixed here. `DualTextureEffect`'s test uses Z∈[0,1] (not the Bgfx/EasyGL siblings' [-0.9,0.9] sweep) since Vulkan's DirectX-convention clip volume clips any primitive with pre-divide Z<0, matching this project's other Vulkan fog tests' established convention. No `RasterizerState::CullNone` needed anywhere (confirmed empirically — Vulkan's default cull state already doesn't cull this test family's quad winding, per the Task 364/896 finding). **Discriminating power independently verified**: `git stash`ed all 13 touched production files (2 `.hpp`/`.cpp` + 9 modified `.glsl` + `compile_shaders.py` + `spirv_shaders.hpp`) plus manually moved aside the 3 new `.glsl` files, regenerated SPIR-V from the reverted sources, rebuilt — all 5 new tests failed 1/3 (fog-off sub-test coincidentally still passes since `fogFactor=1` regardless of the missing infrastructure); restored everything, regenerated, rebuilt, reconfirmed 3/3 on all 5. **Full regression**: `ctest -R Vulkan` 66/78 pass (12 failed, exactly the pre-existing baseline: `Vulkan_BlendState_{AlphaBlend,Additive,SeparateFunctions,SeparateFactors,BlendFactor}`, `Vulkan_DepthStencilState_{CompareFunction,StencilEnable,StencilMask,StencilOps,StencilTwoSided}`, `Vulkan_GraphicsDevice_ReferenceStencil`, `Vulkan_DepthBias` — zero new failures); `CnaTests --gtest_brief=1` 3493 passed / 2 skipped, matching the expected count exactly. `Vulkan_DrawInstanced_3Instances` (the `Instanced3D` landmine above) was caught as a genuine regression on the first full-suite run, root-caused and fixed before this task was considered complete, per the "find and fix the root cause, don't just note it" verification requirement. |
| 899 (Vulkan `env_map3d` leftover) | Add fog to Vulkan's `env_map3d` pipeline — the one genuinely-still-open item the Vulkan core scope row's own writeup flagged as a cheap pickup | ✅ | **Done, closing out every fog gap Task 899 ever touched.** Exactly as predicted: `env_map3d`'s existing `EnvMapParams` UBO (`set=0, binding=2`) had ~160 spare bytes past the 96 originally used (`eyePos`/`diffuseColor`/`emissive+envMapAmount`/`light0Dir`/`light0Diffuse+fresnelEnabled`/`envMapSpecular+fresnelFactor` = 24 floats). Extended `envMapUboData` from `float[24]` to `float[32]` (128 bytes), packing `fogColorEnabled`/`fogStartEnd` at `[24..31]`; `env_map3d.vert.glsl` computes `vFogFactor` from raw object-space `aPos.z` (same established Task 888 formula) and `env_map3d.frag.glsl` mixes toward `fogColorEnabled.xyz` as the final step, mirroring `lit_textured3d.vert.glsl`/`.frag.glsl`'s identical "fog packed into an existing UBO's spare tail" pattern exactly. Both `DrawPrimitivesEx` fill sites (indexed and non-indexed) updated identically; `bufInfo.range` and the `RecordCommandBuffer` memcpy size both bumped 96→128 bytes (`kEnvMapUBOStride=256` has ample room). New `vulkan_environmentmapeffect_fog_test.cpp` (direct port of the Bgfx/EasyGL siblings' 3-subtest shape; no `RasterizerState::CullNone` needed, confirmed empirically against every other Vulkan `EnvironmentMapEffect` test in this family) passed 3/3 on the first attempt. **Discriminating power independently verified**: `git stash`-reverted the `.hpp`/`.cpp`/both `.glsl` files/`spirv_shaders.hpp`, rebuilt, reran — failed 1/3 exactly as predicted (fog-off sub-test coincidentally still passes); restored, rebuilt, reconfirmed 3/3. **Full regression**: `ctest -R Vulkan` 67/79 pass (same 12 pre-existing failures, zero new); `CnaTests --gtest_brief=1` 3493 passed / 2 skipped, unchanged. |
| 899 (Bgfx bonus scope) | Add fog support to Bgfx's `env_map3d`/`skinned3d` pipelines — opportunistic scope explicitly called out in this row's own title | ✅ | **Done, independently of the still-open Vulkan core scope above.** Bgfx's `env_map3d`/`skinned3d` needed zero new C++ infrastructure — `u_fogColor`/`u_fogParams` are already set unconditionally per-draw in `DrawPrimitivesEx` (Task 888), and both effects' `FillGpuDrawParams()` already forward fog fields correctly (Task 900) — only the shader files themselves (`vs`/`fs_env_map3d.sc`, `vs`/`fs_skinned3d.sc`) were missing the fog varying/blend, added mirroring `vs`/`fs_lit_textured3d.sc`'s already-proven pattern exactly (`skinned3d`'s fog factor computed from the **pre-skin** raw object-space Z, matching EasyGL's Task 900 formula). **Found and fixed a real, separate, previously-unreported bug while writing the `SkinnedEffect` fog test**: `SkinnedEffect.EmissiveColor` was a **total GPU no-op on Bgfx** — `fs_skinned3d.sc` never declared or read a `u_emissiveColor` uniform at all (unlike EasyGL's shader, which does), and the C++ dispatch's `skinned` branch in `DrawPrimitivesEx` never set the (already-existing, already-used-elsewhere) `emissiveColor3DUnif_` handle for this branch. This is a distinct gap from Task 894 (`SpecularColor`/`SpecularPower`, still open) — found only because this task's fog test deliberately uses `EmissiveColor` as the sole non-fog material-color signal (mirroring EasyGL's Task 900 test design) to isolate fog cleanly, exactly as Task 886 uncovered Tasks 892/898 the same way. Fixed by adding `u_emissiveColor` to `fs_skinned3d.sc`'s formula (`lit = emissiveColor + ambientColor + NdL*light0Diffuse`, matching EasyGL's already-working combination once accounting for `p.ambientColor` always being 0 for `SkinnedEffect`) and adding the missing `bgfx::setUniform(emissiveColor3DUnif_, emissive)` call to the `skinned` branch. New `bgfx_environmentmapeffect_fog_test.cpp`/`bgfx_skinnedeffect_fog_test.cpp` (direct ports of Task 900's EasyGL tests, `RasterizerState::CullNone` per the established Task 364/896 finding) — `EnvironmentMapEffect` passed 3/3 on the first attempt; `SkinnedEffect` initially failed 1/3 exactly matching the EmissiveColor bug's predicted symptom (pure black instead of blue/purple/red) before the emissive fix, then passed 3/3. All 4 pre-existing `SkinnedEffect` Bgfx tests (`identity_bones`/`translation_bone`/`twobone_blend`/`combined`) re-verified passing unchanged after the emissive fix (all use `EnableDefaultLighting()`, whose light-sum dominates enough that the previously-missing emissive term was never visible before). **Discriminating power independently verified**: `git stash`-reverted all 6 Bgfx production files (4 shader files + `BgfxGraphicsBackend.cpp` + generated `bgfx_shaders.hpp`), rebuilt, reran both new tests — `EnvironmentMapEffect` failed 1/3 (fog OFF case coincidentally still passes since `fogFactor=1` regardless), `SkinnedEffect` failed 0/3 (emissive bug pre-existed independently of fog); restored and reconfirmed both pass 3/3. **Full Bgfx regression**: 47/47 pass, zero failures (no pre-existing Bgfx baseline failures in this run). |
| 900 | Forward fog fields (`FogEnabled`/`FogColor`/`FogStart`/`FogEnd`) in `SkinnedEffect`/`EnvironmentMapEffect`'s `FillGpuDrawParams()` — found during Task 888's research | ✅ | **Real, confirmed gap found and FIXED on EasyGL** (Vulkan/Bgfx unaffected by this task — see below). Both `SkinnedEffect` and `EnvironmentMapEffect` had a complete, correct `IEffectFog` implementation (getters/setters, dirty flags, a properly-computed FNA-style `FogVector` in their own `OnApply()`), but **neither effect's `FillGpuDrawParams()` ever forwarded `FogEnabled`/`FogColor`/`FogStart`/`FogEnd`** — the same architectural gap Task 378/388 already found and fixed for `AlphaTestEffect`/`DualTextureEffect`, never audited for these two. Fixed by mirroring `BasicEffect::FillGpuDrawParams()`'s existing pattern in both effects (`SkinnedEffect.cpp`/`EnvironmentMapEffect.cpp`), reading `FogColor` via each effect's own `getFogColorProperty()` accessor (both effects store it through an `EffectParameter`, `fogColorParam_`, unlike `BasicEffect`'s plain `fogColor_` member — `getFogColorProperty()` already handles this correctly). Also added the fog uniforms/varying/blend logic EasyGL's other 5 shader variants already have (`u/vFogEnabled`/`FogColor`/`FogStart`/`FogEnd`/`FogFactor`, `mix(uFogColor,FragColor.rgb,vFogFactor)` after the existing alpha-test discard) to `EnsureEnvMapped3DProgram()`/`EnsureSkinnedProgram()`'s shaders — both had zero fog code at all before this task, confirmed during Task 888's research. `BindDrawParams()` needed **no changes** — its `loc_fog_*` uniform-setting block (already used by 5 other programs) is fully generic, auto-binding for any program whose `uniform_location()` lookup for `uFogEnabled`/etc. succeeds. New `easygl_{environmentmapeffect,skinnedeffect}_fog_test.cpp` (3 checks each, same fog-off/50%/full-fog structure and exact expected values as `easygl_basiceffect_fog_test.cpp`/Task 195, isolating fog from each effect's reflection/skinning math via `EnvironmentMapAmount=0`+`EnvironmentMapSpecular=0` and an identity bone palette respectively, both using `EmissiveColor` as the controlled material color) — both PASSED on the first attempt with the exact hand-derived expected values. **Discriminating power independently verified**: `git stash`-reverted all 3 production files (`EasyGLGraphicsBackend.cpp`/`SkinnedEffect.cpp`/`EnvironmentMapEffect.cpp`), rebuilt, reran — both new tests failed identically (1/3 PASS each, the fog-disabled case coincidentally passing since `fogFactor=1` regardless, both fog-active cases showing the unblended blue material color instead of any purple-mix/full-red blend); restored and reconfirmed both pass 3/3. **Full EasyGL regression**: 3631/3634 pass — the same 3 pre-existing, already-documented failures (`EasyGL_MRT_TwoAttachments`, `EasyGL_GraphicsDevice_ReferenceStencil`, `easy-gl-resource-smoke-tests`), zero new regressions. **Vulkan/Bgfx are unaffected by this task** — `GpuDrawParams`'s fog fields are now correctly populated by these 2 effects' C++ side, but neither backend's `env_map3d`/`skinned3d` GPU shader pipelines implement fog at all yet (that GPU-side work for these 2 specific pipelines was never in Task 888's scope either, since Task 888 focused on the 3 effects that already forwarded fog correctly before it started); tracked as a natural extension of Task 899's remaining Vulkan work plus equivalent Bgfx `env_map3d`/`skinned3d` fog shader additions, not opened as a further new task number since Task 899's own row already covers `skinned3d` and a `env_map3d` line item can be added there when that work is picked up. |
| 889 | Fix `DualTextureEffect.VertexColorEnabled` being completely ignored on all 3 backends — opened by Task 389 | ⬜ | **Opened while writing Task 389's cross-backend capstone test** — Phase 44 (unlike Phase 43's dedicated Task 377 for `AlphaTestEffect`) never had its own task auditing `VertexColorEnabled` for `DualTextureEffect`, so this went unaudited through Tasks 381–388. Confirmed by source-reading all 3 backends' dual-texture dispatch: **EasyGL**'s `EnsureDualTextured3DProgram()` vertex shader declares only `aPos`+`aUV` (no color attribute at all); **Vulkan**'s `GetOrCreatePipelineDualTex3D()` explicitly reuses `kTextured3dVertSpv` (confirmed via its own comment "reuse kTextured3dVertSpv (reads MVP + diffuseColor from same PC layout)") — the generic textured-only vertex shader, no color attribute; **Bgfx**'s `vs_dual_texture3d.sc` declares only `a_position, a_texcoord0` as inputs, with `v_color0` hardcoded to `u_diffuseColor` (a per-draw uniform broadcast, not a real per-vertex attribute). **`VertexColorEnabled` is a total no-op for `DualTextureEffect` on all 3 backends** — setting it true or false produces byte-identical output everywhere, confirmed by reading (not yet pixel-tested). This is architecturally the same shape as Task 887's already-tracked `AlphaTestEffect` Vulkan/Bgfx vertex-color gap, but here it also affects EasyGL and is `DualTextureEffect`-specific (a completely separate, dedicated shader on every backend, not a shared generic pipeline). Fix shape: add a `VertexPositionColorTexture`-style color attribute (needs its own stride/vertex-format decision, since `DualTextureEffect` currently only supports `VertexPositionTexture`, stride 20, shared across both texture slots' UVs — adding vertex color would need a new stride variant or reusing an existing one with 2 UV sets stripped down to 1) to all 3 backends' dual-texture vertex shaders, multiplying `vin.Color` into the forwarded diffuse per FNA's `VSDualTextureVc`/`VSDualTextureVcNoFog` shader variants (`vout.Diffuse *= vin.Color`). A genuinely new multi-shader-file, multi-backend feature addition, not fixed here. Verification: pixel test with a distinctive vertex color and non-white `DiffuseColor` simultaneously (mirroring Task 377's exact discriminating-value technique), confirming `VertexColorEnabled=true` actually changes the output on all 3 backends once fixed. |
| 890 | Forward `DirectionalLight1`/`DirectionalLight2` on `EnvironmentMapEffect` (all 3 backends) — opened by Task 391 | ⬜ | **Opened by Task 391's audit**, not fixed there (deliberately scoped out, same architectural shape as Task 885's `BasicEffect` finding). Confirmed via `EnvironmentMapEffect.fx` that this effect shares `BasicEffect`'s identical `Lighting.fxh`/`ComputeLights(eyeVector,worldNormal,numLights)` mechanism — the same `oneLight` shader-variant optimization (`shaderIndex+=8` when both `DirectionalLight1`/`DirectionalLight2` are disabled) and up-to-3-light support exists in real FNA for this effect too. CNA's `EnvironmentMapEffect::FillGpuDrawParams()` only forwards `DirectionalLight0`'s direction/diffuse (`p.light0Dir`/`p.light0Diffuse`) — `DirectionalLight1`/`DirectionalLight2` are silently ignored by every backend's GPU dispatch today, confirmed unchanged since the effect's original implementation. Fix shape: closely related to Task 885 — needs new `GpuDrawParams` fields for light1/2 direction+diffuse, `FillGpuDrawParams()` forwarding (with the same `Enabled`-gating fix Task 368 applied to `BasicEffect`'s light0), and each backend's env-map lit shader (`vs/fs_env_map3d`) summing all 3 lights' contributions. Likely shares Task 885's exact `GpuDrawParams`/shader-summing plumbing and, on Vulkan, its push-constant budget concerns — consider doing both together rather than duplicating the light-summing shader logic twice. Verification: pixel test with 2+ lights enabled simultaneously on `EnvironmentMapEffect`, values chosen so each light's individual contribution is separately distinguishable (mirrors Task 885's own planned verification). |
| 891 | Scale `EnvironmentMapEffect`'s cube-map lerp target (`envColor`) by combined texture×diffuse alpha on all 3 backends — opened by Task 395 | ⬜ | **Opened by Task 395's fix**, not fixed there (deliberately scoped out to keep that task's specular-only fix isolated and cleanly testable). FNA's real `PSEnvMap`/`PSEnvMapSpecular` pixel shaders compute `envmap = SAMPLE_CUBEMAP(EnvironmentMap, pin.EnvCoord) * color.a;` where `color.a = texColor.a * DiffuseColor.a` — this single multiply scales **both** `envmap.rgb` (used in `color.rgb = lerp(color.rgb, envmap.rgb, pin.Specular.rgb)`, the base cube-map blend) **and** `envmap.a` (used in `EnvironmentMapSpecular`'s multiply, already fixed by Task 395). CNA's fixed shaders now correctly scale the specular term by `envSample.a × combinedAlpha`, but the base lerp's `envColor`/`envSample.rgb` term is still used **unscaled** — `mix(baseColor, envSample.rgb, Amount)` instead of FNA's real `mix(baseColor, envSample.rgb * combinedAlpha, Amount)`. This only produces a visible difference when `texColor.a × DiffuseColor.a` is strictly less than 1 (every existing env-map test across Tasks 134/136/192/278/393/394/395 deliberately used fully opaque textures/diffuse colors, so this nuance has never been exercised or exposed by any test to date). Fix shape: trivial, same-shape change to the same 3 shader files already touched by Task 395 (`EnsureEnvMapped3DProgram()`, `env_map3d.frag.glsl`, `fs_env_map3d.sc`) — multiply `envSample.rgb`/`.xyz` by `combinedAlpha` before passing it to `mix()`. Verification: pixel test with a translucent (non-1-alpha) texture or non-1 `Alpha` property, `EnvironmentMapAmount` between 0 and 1 or exactly 1, confirming the cube map's contribution is itself alpha-scaled (mirrors Task 395's own translucent-cubemap discriminating technique, but scaling the *texture/diffuse* alpha instead of the *cubemap's* alpha). |
| 892 | Fix `BasicEffect`'s lit-textured normal transform on Bgfx — transforms the normal by the full `u_wvp` (World×View×Projection) matrix instead of `World`'s inverse-transpose — opened by Task 398 | ✅ | **Fixed exactly as predicted by this row's own prior analysis** — discovered as a hard blocker while writing Task 886's real-camera Bgfx specular test (the first Bgfx `BasicEffect` lit-textured test to ever use a non-identity `View`/`Projection`; every prior test, Tasks 367-885, used identity matrices, which made `u_wvp≈World`/`Identity` and completely masked this bug). Symptom before the fix: the test's diffuse-only baseline check (expected `(48,48,48)`) instead read `(2,2,2)` — pure ambient, meaning `NdotL` was evaluating to ~0 regardless of the configured light, because the WVP-corrupted normal was geometrically meaningless. Fixed by adding a `u_normalMatrix` (Mat3) uniform to `vs_lit_textured3d.sc`, computed via the same CPU-side cofactor/det shortcut already used for `EnvironmentMapEffect` (`ComputeNormalMatrix3x3()`, reused directly — no new C++ helper needed), bound via the pre-existing generic `normalMatrix3DUnif_` handle. New dedicated test `examples/bgfx_basiceffect_normaltransform_test.cpp` (diffuse-only, `SpecularColor=(0,0,0)`, deliberately isolated from Task 886's own specular feature) with a real camera via `Matrix::CreateLookAt`+`CreatePerspectiveFieldOfView`, `World=Identity` (proving the bug needs only a non-identity *camera*, not non-uniform World scale — a stricter/more severe bug shape than Task 398's `EnvironmentMapEffect` one). **Discriminating power independently verified**: temporarily reverted just the `v_normal` computation line back to the WVP-based formula (leaving all of Task 886's other changes in place), rebuilt, reran — reproduced the exact predicted `(2,2,2)` failure; restored and reconfirmed the fix. Committed together with Task 886 (a genuine prerequisite for that task's own Bgfx test to be meaningful, not an incidental unrelated fix). |
| 893 | Forward `DirectionalLight1`/`DirectionalLight2` on `SkinnedEffect` (all 3 backends) — opened by Task 401 | ⬜ | **Opened by Task 401's audit**, not fixed there (deliberately scoped out, same architectural shape as Tasks 885/890's `BasicEffect`/`EnvironmentMapEffect` findings). Confirmed via `SkinnedEffect.fx` that this effect shares the identical `Lighting.fxh`/`ComputeLights(eyeVector,worldNormal,numLights)` mechanism (same `oneLight` shader-variant optimization, same up-to-3-light support in real FNA). CNA's `SkinnedEffect::FillGpuDrawParams()` only forwards `DirectionalLight0`'s direction/diffuse — `DirectionalLight1`/`DirectionalLight2` are silently ignored by every backend's GPU dispatch today. Fix shape: identical to Tasks 885/890 — needs new `GpuDrawParams` fields for light1/2 direction+diffuse (if not already added by whichever of 885/890 lands first — strongly consider doing all 3 effects' light1/2 forwarding together in one pass, since they'd share the exact same `GpuDrawParams`/shader-summing plumbing and, on Vulkan, the same push-constant budget concerns already flagged by Task 885), `FillGpuDrawParams()` forwarding with `Enabled`-gating, and each backend's skinned lit shader summing all 3 lights' contributions. Verification: pixel test with 2+ lights enabled simultaneously on `SkinnedEffect`, values chosen so each light's individual contribution is separately distinguishable (mirrors Task 885/890's own planned verification). |
| 894 | Implement real specular highlights for `SkinnedEffect` (all 3 backends) — opened by Task 401 | ⬜ | **Opened by Task 401's audit**, not fixed there (a new feature, not a bug fix — the identical shape as `BasicEffect`'s already-tracked Task 886, extended here to a second effect). **Confirmed zero specular GPU infrastructure exists for `SkinnedEffect` on any backend**: `GpuDrawParams` has no generic `specularColor`/`specularPower` fields at all (only the unrelated, much simpler `envMapSpecular` flat-tint field used by `EnvironmentMapEffect`); `SkinnedEffect::FillGpuDrawParams()` never reads `getSpecularColorProperty()`/`getSpecularPowerProperty()` at all. FNA reference: identical `Lighting.fxh`'s `ComputeLights()` Blinn-Phong formula already documented in Task 886's own row (`halfVector`, `pow(...,SpecularPower)`, `AddSpecular`). Given `SkinnedEffect` and `BasicEffect` share the exact same lighting math and specular formula in real FNA, **strongly consider implementing both effects' specular support together in one task** rather than duplicating the half-vector/eye-position/`pow()` shader infrastructure twice across 2 separate tasks. On Vulkan, likely needs the same push-constant expansion already flagged by Task 885/886. Verification: pixel test with a strong, deliberately non-degenerate specular highlight, mirroring Task 886's own planned verification technique. |
| 895 | Fix `SkinnedEffect.WeightsPerVertex` being a complete GPU no-op on all 3 backends — opened by Task 401 | ⬜ | **Opened by Task 401's audit**, not fixed there — a real, confirmed, project-wide (all 3 backends) divergence from FNA's actual per-vertex-weight-count semantics. FNA's real `Skin(vin, boneCount)` HLSL function (`SkinnedEffect.fx`) only sums the **first `boneCount`** weight/index pairs: `[unroll] for (int i=0;i<boneCount;i++) skinning += Bones[vin.Indices[i]]*vin.Weights[i];` — for `WeightsPerVertex=1`, only `Weights[0]`/`Indices[0]` contribute at all; the remaining 3 weight/index slots are never read, regardless of their stored values. CNA's skinning vertex shader on **all 3 backends** (EasyGL's `aBoneWeights`, Vulkan's `aBoneWeights`, Bgfx's `a_weight`) unconditionally sums **all 4** weight/index pairs every time, confirmed via direct shader-source reading — `WeightsPerVertex`'s setter/validation (1/2/4, throws otherwise) and its `EffectDirtyFlags.ShaderIndex`-driven `shaderIndex` bit (`+2`/`+4`) are both correctly implemented on the C++ side and correctly forwarded to the `ShaderIndex` `EffectParameter` (matching FNA's own formula exactly, confirmed by Task 401), but **no backend's actual GPU dispatch branches on this shader-index bit at all** — there is only ever one skinning shader per backend, always behaving as `WeightsPerVertex=4`. Only produces a visible rendering difference from FNA when a vertex's "unused" weight slots (beyond the declared `WeightsPerVertex` count) hold genuinely nonzero data instead of `0` — realistic authored/exported skinned content typically zeros unused slots, so this may be practically invisible for well-formed models, but is still a real, testable, confirmed divergence. Fix shape: either (a) add 3 real shader variants per backend (1/2/4-weight, matching FNA's own `VSSkinnedVertexLightingOneBone`/`TwoBones`/`FourBones` variant split), selected via the existing shader-index dispatch machinery, or (b) the pragmatically simpler fix — mask/zero the unused weight slots on the CPU side (or in a single shared shader) based on `WeightsPerVertex` before the GPU sum, achieving the same net numerical result without a full shader-variant split. Verification: pixel test with deliberately nonzero "garbage" data in weight slots beyond the declared `WeightsPerVertex` count, confirming CNA currently (incorrectly) includes their contribution while FNA would not. |
| 896 | Fix `GraphicsDevice`'s default `RasterizerState` never being pushed to any backend's actual GPU state at construction — split out of Task 884's original title, opened by Task 364 | ✅ | **Closed, per the user's own scoping decision (full upfront winding-order audit before landing the fix, not a lighter reactive approach — see `project_task883_896_decisions.md`).** By the time this task started, the affected set had grown to 119 `examples/*.cpp` files (up from the original 128-of-208 estimate as more examples were added between sessions). Audited/fixed via 8 parallel agent forks, split into two methodologies: **(1) mechanical mirror (64 files, 4 forks)** — every `easygl_X_test.cpp`/`vulkan_X_test.cpp` whose `bgfx_X_test.cpp` sibling already carried the `RasterizerState::CullNone` fix (proven necessary by Bgfx's already-correct default) got the identical fix mirrored in, no independent judgment needed. **(2) real winding audit (55 files, 4 forks)** — files with no Bgfx sibling (mostly EasyGL-only feature tests: blend/depth-stencil state, buffer/draw-call edge cases, texture filter/address-mode tests) got their actual triangle vertex data read and classified via a pre-calibrated 2D cross-product formula (matching this codebase's confirmed CW-front/CCW-back convention — see `VulkanGraphicsBackend.cpp`'s pipeline-creation comment): `cross = (v1.x-v0.x)*(v2.y-v0.y) - (v1.y-v0.y)*(v2.x-v0.x)`, `cross > 0` (CCW) → needs `CullNone`, `cross < 0` (CW) → already safe. Result: 115 of 119 files needed and got the fix (matching precedent: add `RasterizerState::CullNone` at the draw site rather than rewrite vertex data), 4 were confirmed genuinely safe with no change (pure exception-guard tests with no real/degenerate geometry). One file (`house3d_demo.cpp`, an interactive 3D demo, not a pass/fail test) was only spot-checked (3 of 6 box faces) by its fork — **independently re-verified by hand afterward**: derived the general 3D rule for this codebase (a face is front-facing/safe only if its right-hand-rule normal points *toward* the box center, i.e. *inward* — the opposite of the usual OpenGL-style outward-normal convention, a direct consequence of this codebase's CW-front default) and confirmed all 6 of `AddBox()`'s faces produce outward normals via its uniform per-face vertex-ordering template, meaning the single global `CullNone` call the fork added is correct and complete, not just a 3-face guess. Also surfaced (not a defect, just noted): 2 pure-smoke-test Bgfx files (`bgfx_env_map_test.cpp`, `bgfx_render_target_cube_sample_test.cpp`) turned out to already be CCW/silently-culled under Bgfx's own pre-existing correct default — invisible until now because neither test asserts real pixel values, only "didn't crash." One mechanical-mirror fork used the wrong `GraphicsDevice&` variable name in `vulkan_dual_texture_test.cpp` (`dev` instead of the file's actual `device`) — caught immediately by the very next full-solution build (100% clean across all 3 backends otherwise) and fixed. **Landed the actual one-line fix** in `GraphicsDevice`'s ctor: `setRasterizerStateProperty(rasterizerState_)` right after `createBackend()`/`UpdateViewportFromWindow()`, syncing all 3 backends' real GPU cull state to FNA's actual default (`CullCounterClockwiseFace`) from construction onward. **Verification**: all 3 backends build 100% clean (zero compiler errors across the 114 modified files + the 1-line ctor change); full `CnaTests` regression clean on all 3 (EasyGL 3501/2 skipped, Vulkan 3501/2 skipped, Bgfx 3505/2 skipped — all exact baseline matches, 0 new failures); spot-ran ~12 individual example binaries spanning every fix category (mechanical-mirror, real-audit-fixed, real-audit-safe, the flagged demo) — all passed except the already-known, independently-reconfirmed-pre-existing `EasyGL_MRT_TwoAttachments` (Task 145, verified via `git stash` to fail identically on the pre-Task-896 codebase, completely unrelated to culling). No other regressions found. |
| 897 | Expand Vulkan's push-constant/UBO budget to forward `BasicEffect`'s `DirectionalLight1`/`DirectionalLight2`/`EmissiveColor` on the lit-textured path — split out of Task 885, opened by Task 369 | ✅ | **Implemented exactly as scoped**, mirroring `EnvironmentMapEffect`'s own small-UBO precedent. Added a fully dedicated Vulkan resource set for the lit-textured (stride-32) pipeline: `descriptorSetLayoutLitTextured_` (`set=0`: binding 0 = `sampler2D` texture, binding 1 = `VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_UNIFORM_BUFFER_DYNAMIC` `LitLightParams` — 5 padded `vec4`s: `light1Dir_pad`/`light1Diffuse_pad`/`light2Dir_pad`/`light2Diffuse_pad`/`emissiveColor_pad`, fragment-stage only since lighting math is entirely per-pixel), `pipelineLayoutLitTextured3D_` (keeping the *same unchanged* 128-byte push-constant range/content as `pipelineLayoutExt3D_` — `FillExtPushConst()` still fills it, byte-for-byte identical to before), `pipelinesLitTextured3D_` cache, and a 512-slot per-frame dynamic-offset UBO ring buffer (`litTexturedUBO_`/`kLitTexturedUBOStride=256`/`kLitTexturedUBOMaxDraws=512`, mirroring `envMapUBO_` exactly). Added `EnsureLitTexturedResources()`/`GetOrCreateLitTexturedDescSet()`/`GetOrCreatePipelineLitTextured3D()`, mirroring `EnsureEnvMapResources()`/`GetOrCreateEnvMapDescSet()`/`GetOrCreatePipelineEnvMap3D()` line-for-line. `DrawPrimitivesEx()`/`DrawIndexedPrimitivesEx()` gained a `needsLitTextured = (stride==32) && !alphaTest && !dualTex && !envMap && !skinned` condition (the exact pre-existing condition that already implied "stride-32 lit-textured shader" via `GetOrCreatePipelineExt3D`'s internal switch, just now given its own explicit flag) and populate `d.litUboData`/`d.litTexturedDescSet`; `d.useExtParams` now explicitly excludes this case so strides 20/24 and `Instanced3D` are completely untouched, still routing through the original, unmodified `pipelineLayoutExt3D_`/`useExtParams` path. `RecordCommandBuffer`'s `draw3DFor` lambda gained a `useLitTextured` branch in both the pipeline-selection and push-constant/descriptor-set-binding switches (with its own `litTexturedUBOSlot` counter, mirroring `envMapUBOSlot`/`skinnedUBOSlot`) — deliberately **no `defaultWhiteDescSet_` fallback** here (unlike `useExtParams`/`useAlphaTest`/`useDualTexture`), since that set belongs to the structurally-incompatible 1-binding `descriptorSetLayout_`; mirrors `useEnvMap`/`useSkinned`'s own no-fallback pattern instead. `lit_textured3d.frag.glsl` extended with the new UBO block and the identical 3-light-sum + emissive formula already verified correct on EasyGL/Bgfx (Task 885): `litColor = v_color0.rgb*finalLight + emissiveColor` computed *before* the texture multiply, matching FNA's `PSBasicTexture: tex2D(...) * pin.Diffuse` (texture modulates the whole diffuse+emissive sum, but `DiffuseColor` doesn't re-scale `EmissiveColor` a second time). `lit_textured3d.vert.glsl` unchanged (no UBO needed in the vertex stage). Regenerated `spirv_shaders.hpp` via `compile_shaders.py` (uses `libshaderc` directly). New test `examples/vulkan_basiceffect_multilight_emissive_test.cpp` (direct port of Task 885's EasyGL test, same 3 checks, no `RasterizerState::CullNone` workaround needed — Vulkan's cull default is effectively `CullNone`, matching EasyGL, Task 896 finding) passed on the very first attempt with the exact hand-derived expected values, confirming the new pipeline/UBO infrastructure works correctly. **Discriminating power independently verified**: `git stash`-reverted all 4 changed Vulkan-specific files (header/cpp/shader/generated SPIR-V header), rebuilt, reran — all 3 checks failed identically with the exact predicted pre-fix value `(89,13,13)` (ambient+light0-only, matching EasyGL/Bgfx's own pre-fix result byte-for-byte); restored and reconfirmed all 3 checks pass. **Full Vulkan regression** (serial, `-j1`, to rule out parallel-execution GPU-context flakiness): 3546/3559 pass — the 13 failures (`Vulkan_BlendState_*`×5, `Vulkan_DepthStencilState_*`×5, `Vulkan_GraphicsDevice_ReferenceStencil`, `Vulkan_DepthBias`, 1 flaky `CueTest`) were independently confirmed **pre-existing and unrelated**: reran the exact same 12 graphics failures with all of this task's Vulkan changes `git stash`-reverted, and every one failed identically without any of this task's code present, proving they predate this task entirely (a known, already-documented baseline gap, not a regression). This closes the `BasicEffect` `DirectionalLight1`/`DirectionalLight2`/`EmissiveColor` lit-path gap on all 3 backends (Tasks 885 + 897 together). |
| 898 | Fix Vulkan's `lit_textured3d.vert.glsl` transforming the vertex normal by the full MVP matrix instead of World's inverse-transpose — discovered as a hard blocker for Task 886's own real-camera specular test, Vulkan analog of Task 892 | ✅ | **Real, pre-existing, previously-invisible bug**, identical in shape to Task 892's Bgfx bug: `fragNormal = normalize(mat3(pc.mvp) * inNormal);` bakes View/Projection into the surface normal, which is wrong under any non-identity camera (not just non-uniform World scale) — completely masked by every prior Vulkan lit-textured test's use of identity View/Projection. Surfaced while building Task 886's Vulkan specular test, which (unlike earlier lit-textured tests) requires a real, non-degenerate camera via `Matrix::CreateLookAt`/`CreatePerspectiveFieldOfView` so that `EyePosition` (derived from `Matrix::Invert(View).Translation`) is a genuine off-plane point. Symptom matched Task 892's exactly: the diffuse-only check expected `(48,48,48)` but read `(2,2,2)` (pure ambient — `NdotL` collapsing to ~0 regardless of the configured light) once a real camera was introduced. **Fixed** in `lit_textured3d.vert.glsl` using GLSL's built-in `inverse()` computed directly in-shader — `mat3 normalMatrix = transpose(inverse(mat3(lp.world))); fragNormal = normalize(normalMatrix * inNormal);` — mirroring `env_map3d.vert.glsl`'s own already-correct in-shader pattern (Vulkan's GLSL 450 has `inverse()` built in, so unlike EasyGL/Bgfx there was no need for a CPU-side `ComputeNormalMatrix3x3`-equivalent helper). Required extending the `LitLightParams` UBO (`set=0, binding=1`, previously fragment-stage-only from Task 897) with a `mat4 world` field and promoting the descriptor binding's `stageFlags` to `VK_SHADER_STAGE_VERTEX_BIT | VK_SHADER_STAGE_FRAGMENT_BIT`, plus adding a new `fragWorldPos` varying (`location = 3`) so the fragment shader's specular half-vector (`E = normalize(eyePos - fragWorldPos)`, Task 886) has a correct world-space position to work from — both changes bundled with Task 886/897's own UBO extension in the same commit since they touch the identical struct/binding. **Discriminating power independently verified**: full `git stash` revert-and-rebuild of all Vulkan-specific changed files (header, cpp, both `lit_textured3d` shaders, generated `spirv_shaders.hpp`), reran `Vulkan_BasicEffect_Specular` — reproduced the exact predicted pre-fix failure pattern `(2,2,2)`/`(18,18,18)`/`(2,2,2)`/`(2,2,2)` across all 4 checks (specular contribution collapsing to ~0 in every case since `NdotL` itself was corrupted); restored and reconfirmed all 4 checks pass. **Full Vulkan regression** (serial, `-j1`): 3548/3560 pass, the same 12 pre-existing, already-documented failures (`Vulkan_BlendState_*`×5, `Vulkan_DepthStencilState_*`×5, `Vulkan_GraphicsDevice_ReferenceStencil`, `Vulkan_DepthBias`) — independently reconfirmed unrelated to this task by reproducing them identically with this task's changes `git stash`-reverted. Closes the Vulkan-side normal-transform gap opened alongside Task 892's Bgfx-side fix; EasyGL's lit-textured vertex shader was already correct (computed the normal via a proper world-space transform from the start, never went through an MVP-based shortcut). |

## Phase 47 — SpriteBatch renderer correctness

| #   | Task                                                                     | Status | Notes                 |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------ | --------------------- |
| 664 | Fix Vulkan: multiple `SpriteBatch::Begin()`/`End()` calls per frame — only the last batch renders | ✅ | **Real, confirmed bug, root cause isolated and fixed** — instrumented/traced the Vulkan command recording first, per this row's own prior instruction, rather than guess-fixing. **Root cause (2 compounding mechanisms)**: (1) `VulkanSpriteBatchBackend::Begin()` unconditionally cleared its own `vertices_`/`indices_`/`draws_` member vectors — the *same* mutable storage a prior `End()` had just finished populating. `activeBatches_` (owned by `VulkanGraphicsBackend`) tracked entries by **raw pointer** to that single object, pushed at `Begin()` time. Since the once-per-frame harvest (`RecordCommandBuffer`, driven by `Present()`) runs strictly after all of a frame's `Begin/Draw/End` calls, a 2nd `Begin()` call destroyed the 1st cycle's already-completed geometry *before* the harvest ever got a chance to read it — by the time any Vulkan command was even recorded, the 1st batch's data no longer existed in process memory. (2) A second, latent, previously-masked bug in `drawSpritesFor`'s harvest loop: every `activeBatches_` entry was `memcpy`'d to a **hardcoded offset 0** in the shared per-frame `spriteVB_`/`spriteIB_` buffers (unlike the already-correct 3D immediate-draw path, `draw3DFor`, which uses a genuine accumulating `vbOff`/`ibOff` cursor) — so even after fixing (1), 2 *genuinely distinct* batches targeting the same render target in one frame would still stomp on each other's GPU-visible memory before `vkQueueSubmit` ran. **Fix**: added `VulkanSpriteBatchBackend::BatchSnapshot` (owns `vertices`/`indices`/`draws`/`customEffectBackend` by value); `End()` now moves the completed cycle's geometry into a freshly heap-allocated snapshot and pushes it onto `activeBatches_` (changed to `std::vector<std::pair<std::unique_ptr<BatchSnapshot>, VulkanRTSource*>>`) — not `Begin()` — so a 2nd `Begin()` call only ever resets its own not-yet-used working vectors, never a completed cycle's already-snapshotted data. Gave `drawSpritesFor` a genuine accumulating `vbOff`/`ibOff` cursor mirroring `draw3DFor`'s exact pattern (bounds-checked against `MaxSpriteVertices`/`MaxSpriteIndices`, `memcpy`'d and bound at the running offset, advanced by each snapshot's byte size after its draw calls are recorded) so multiple snapshots targeting the same RT now compose additively instead of overwriting each other. Removed the now-unnecessary `ConsumeDraws()`/`GetVertices()`/`GetIndices()`/`GetDrawCalls()`/`GetCustomEffectBackend()` accessors (snapshots are read directly by the harvest loop; destroyed en masse via the existing once-per-frame `activeBatches_.clear()`). Added `activeRT_` member to `VulkanSpriteBatchBackend`, captured at `Begin()` (preserving the original "RT active during this cycle" semantics) and used when pushing the snapshot at `End()`. **New regression test** `examples/vulkan_spritebatch_multi_begin_end_test.cpp`: 2 independent `Begin()`/`Draw()`/`End()` cycles in one `Draw(GameTime)` (Red quad left half, Blue quad right half, exactly matching the confirmed repro) — **both regions PASS with the exact predicted colors on the first attempt after the fix**. **Discriminating power independently verified**: `git stash`-reverted both changed files (header+cpp) and rebuilt — the pre-fix code reproduced the exact reported symptom (`left region = black/clear (0,0,0)` instead of Red, right region correctly Blue) confirming the test genuinely discriminates the bug; `git stash pop`-restored and reconfirmed both regions pass. Full Vulkan rebuild + regression pass: ctest 3543/3555 (12 of the 13 previously-documented pre-existing failures reproduced exact-name-match; `Vulkan_RenderTargetCube_SampleAfterUnbind` — Task 876's own already-documented flaky/non-deterministic pre-existing failure — passed this run, reran 5× in isolation and passed every time, confirming it's unrelated to this fix, not a regression; +1 new test). |
| 665 | Fix Vulkan: `SpriteBatch::Begin()`'s `SamplerState` (Filter/AddressU/AddressV) has no effect | ✅ | **Real, confirmed bug found and fixed — confirmed BOTH predicted root causes present, not just one.** (1) `VulkanSpriteBatchBackend` didn't override `SetSamplerFilter`/`SetSamplerAddressMode` at all (silent no-op via `ISpriteBatchBackend`'s default empty bodies); `FlushTexture()` always used the texture's own pre-baked, fixed-at-load-time descriptor set (`currentTexture_->GetVkDescriptorSet()`), completely bypassing the per-slot `VkSampler` system entirely. (2) `Draw()` separately `std::clamp`ed the CPU-computed UVs to `[0,1]` regardless of the actual `sourceRectangle` extent — exactly the second bug this row predicted by analogy to Task 269's EasyGL fix — silently defeating `Wrap`/`Mirror` addressing even if bug (1) were fixed alone, since a clamped UV can never leave `[0,1]` for any sampler to wrap/mirror. **Fixed both**: added `SetSamplerFilter`/`SetSamplerAddressMode` overrides storing `pendingFilter_`/`pendingAddressU_`/`pendingAddressV_` (mirroring `EasyGLSpriteBatchBackend`'s exact field names/defaults — `SamplerState.LinearClamp`, `SpriteBatch`'s own real default); `FlushTexture()` now calls `backend_->ApplySamplerState(0, pendingFilter_, pendingAddressU_, pendingAddressV_, 1)` (Task 118's existing per-slot cache) then builds a fresh descriptor set via `backend_->GetOrCreateTexSamplerDescSet(currentTexture_->GetVkImageView(), backend_->slotSamplers_[0])` combining the texture's own image view with the *current* slot-0 sampler, instead of the texture's fixed one. Removed the erroneous `std::clamp(...,0.f,1.f)` calls in `Draw()`'s UV computation, matching FNA's real unclamped `SpriteBatch.cs` behavior (a `sourceRectangle` extending past texture bounds intentionally produces out-of-`[0,1]` UVs — the classic XNA scrolling/tiling technique). **New test** `examples/vulkan_texture_address_mode_test.cpp` — a direct 1:1 port of Task 269's own `easygl_texture_address_mode_test.cpp` (identical 2×1 Red/Blue texture, identical `sourceRectangle` 2× texture width, identical `U≈1.25` read-back point, identical `PointWrap`-vs-`PointClamp` comparison): **both checks pass with the exact predicted colors on the first attempt** (`PointWrap→Red`, `PointClamp→Blue`). **Discriminating power independently verified**: `git stash`-reverted both changed files and rebuilt — the pre-fix code produced the exact predicted symptom, both `PointWrap` and `PointClamp` reading the **identical** blended color `(64,0,191)` (a bilinear-filtered boundary blend from the fixed default sampler, proving `Wrap` addressing genuinely never took effect either way); `git stash pop`-restored and reconfirmed both checks pass. Full Vulkan rebuild + regression pass: ctest 3544/3556 (same 12 of 13 previously-documented pre-existing failures as Task 664's run, exact-name match, zero new regressions; +1 new test). |
